des $\Sigma_{\alpha}^{+}$ ($\alpha\in K$) et de
\[

\]

## Brief attributed note

The manuscript writes the opening target $\overline V\cap E_\beta=\varnothing$ and then writes “mieux, je dis que” before a single-alpha inclusion. Its marginal note says “C'est trivial” and includes a cancelled unreadable item. Underlined Lemme and Corollaire headings organize the next assertions; the corollary names the partition $\Pi_K^\circ$, with $\Pi_K^0$ retained as the complete alternative. The final sentence reads “Immédiat par récurrence sur $\operatorname{card} K\geqslant 0$,” with both accent-qualified words preserved.

The sheet corrects contient to raised rencontre, cancels donc si, tel que les and et, and replaces the cancelled E_i est contenu/contenue clause by a raised existential whose que has a qualified cancellation target. Its unfinished Cela line retains precise, précise, provient, precise le and précise le before firm du. The repeated relation, crowded family index, decorated Sigma glyphs and shared formée/cup strokes remain as transcribed. The written hyp., préc. and card remain abbreviated. Archive number22 appears only in the source comment. These are attributed manuscript readings; no mathematical check event was performed.

## Abbreviations

hyp. — probable hypothèses
préc. — probable précédentes
card — probable cardinal
